# v8 timeline: Screen_Recording_2026-10-31_at_16.31.33
60 fps analysis grid; 3627 frames; 1622x918 (picture cropped from the 1622x936 screen recording); classes: GRAPHICS 11, MIXED 16, LIVE 0.
Screen recording of a 60 s product film playing in the X player: variable frame rate on the 60 Hz grid (1981 pictures, mostly 2 grid frames apart, with 3:2 runs of 24 fps material and runs of 1-frame intervals; motion.csv local_fps 22.6-45.2), so every duration carries +-1 analysis frame of capture jitter and the 2-3 frame quantisation of the captured cadence. Player overlays are ignored: the speaker icon at the bottom right and, from f3546, a 1-px dashed focus ring along the picture's left, right and bottom edges.
Merged from chunks v8c1 (f0-f733), v8c2 (f734-f1461), v8c3 (f1462-f2192), v8c4 (f2193-f2897) and v8c5 (f2898-f3626). Every chunk edge falls inside a segment, checked at the merge on the overview sheets: f733/f734 inside the sidebar close-up (sheets/overview_00720-00765.jpg: the mascot rests in the list card, the card title fades in from f741), f1461/f1462 inside the checklist punch-in (sheets/overview_01440-01485.jpg: the block drifts up to f1468, hard cut f1470), f2192/f2193 inside the 3D stack with callouts (sheets/overview_02160-02205.jpg: the callouts keep collapsing), f2897/f2898 inside the centred phone card (sheets/overview_02880-02925.jpg: the card holds, the tap comes at about f2919). So S06, S11, S17 and S22 are stitched from two fragments each: their prose fields give each fragment's frame range, and the second fragment's table rows follow the first's.
Footage, counted as every chunk counted it: the masked footage card and the full-frame footage (S02-S03, S23-S25), the photographs and still photo panels inside post cards, thread panels, product pages and the 3D screen stack (S08-S10, S12-S14, S16-S18), and the screen recording carried by the phone card (S21-S22); these segments are MIXED and only their graphic layers are analysed. No segment is LIVE: the two footage-only stretches, f452-f511 (after the prompt pill drops out, S03) and f3092-f3163 (after the zoom-fill, S24), sit inside MIXED segments with no boundary to separate them (sheets/overview_00432-00477.jpg, sheets/overview_00480-00525.jpg, sheets/overview_03072-03117.jpg, sheets/overview_03120-03165.jpg).

Boundaries: T01 f115-f143 zoom-through into a cross-dissolve (soft) · T02 f164-f200 tunnel-exit zoom-through, footage card from f174 (soft) · T03 hard cut f512 · T04 f570-f634 blur whip-reframe, hidden cut f608, zoom-out x0.66 (soft) · T05 f668-f726 blur whip-reframe, hidden cut f699, punch-in x2.64 (soft) · T06 f887-f934 whip up, hidden cut f914 (soft) · T07 f980-f1030 whip up, blur-through variant, hidden cut f1004 (soft) · T08 hard cut f1266, punch-in x2.5 · T09 hard cut f1327 on leftward motion · T10 hard cut f1422, punch-in x2.38 · T11 hard cut f1470 into a whip landing · T12 f1648-f1696 push up with staggered card columns (soft) · T13 f1800-f1857 whip down, hidden cut f1829 (soft) · T14 f1900-f1953 whip left, hidden cut f1927 (soft) · T15 f1962-f2008 push up (soft) · T16 f2009-f2035 zoom-out morph into a 3D screen stack (soft) · T17 f2212-f2258 stack push-in and flatten, morph (soft) · T18 f2367-f2440 push up, hidden cut f2404 (soft) · T19 f2478-f2522 avatar fly-and-split, morph (soft) · T20 f2604-f2670 mascot converge-and-fly, morph (soft) · T21 f2800-f2860 card zoom to the centre, morph (soft) · T22 f3029-f3064 push up (soft) · T23 f3064-f3092 zoom-fill, morph (soft) · T24 hard cut f3242 · T25 f3348-f3394 whip down with a hidden two-step cut, background f3379, foreground f3380 (soft) · T26 hard cut f3561, the player's loop restart (not designed). 7 hard cuts and 19 soft transitions. In cuts.json 17 detector candidates are confirmed (the anchors of 5 hard cuts and 12 soft transitions), 80 are rejected (camera moves inside footage, typing, card-deck flips, scrolls, motion blur, detector index drift) and the two hard cuts the detectors missed are the manual entries m001 (f1422) and m002 (f3561); 7 soft transitions (T04, T05, T06, T18, T19, T20, T21) have no anchor candidate (only c075 fired inside T21, on its settle) and were found on the sheets, in motion.csv and in the trackers. Segment edges follow the boundary rule (a soft transition [a, b] ends the previous segment at floor((a+b)/2)), so where the switch picture is off the midpoint, the last outgoing pictures open the next segment (f130-f135, f603-f607, f698, f911-f913, f3372-f3379) or the first incoming pictures close the previous one (f174-f182, f1004-f1005).
Rhythm: 117.45 BPM = 30.65 f per beat (audio.json; the capture's audio delay is unknown, so offsets carry it). Picture changes sit near the grid without locking to it: hard cuts f512 (+1), f1266 (-1), f1327 (+3), f1422 (+6), f1470 (-3), f3242 (+0); hidden cuts f1004 (-1), f1829 (-8), f1927 (+7), f2404 (+0), f3380 (-1); the S06 list steps peak 15 f after their beats (the off-beat); the S05 status shimmer loops every 62-63 f, about 2 beats.
Look: light page (luma 246-248) in every UI segment; opening and end card on a light field (247) with two soft darker blobs (224 / 218) and a faint static dot grid. UI ink: settled text near black (darkest pixel 0-22), light-grey status words (darkest pixel about 116-160), mascot fills 38-221 (dark 94, mid 147-149, light 221), status pills and list cards 231, ghost card band 224, composer and prompt pill white with a black round mic button. Dark UI blocks (post cards, thread panel, product page, phone card) carry light text. Motion blur is directional and tied to speed: it appears on whatever moves fast and clears within about 6-17 frames of a landing.
Typefaces: UNCERTAIN: f0-f3626 because the original UI and wordmark typefaces cannot be identified from the grayscale capture; the closest free family is Inter for UI text and wordmarks (Inter Tight for some headings), weights judged visually.
Merge decisions: v8c1's `v8.cam-slow-push` (S01) is the same device as v8c5's `v8.layout-scale-creep` (S26): over f30-f112 the background's best-fit scale stays x1.000 while the lockup fits x1.016 (scratch/merge_S01_creep_bgfit_30-112.csv), so both are listed as `v8.layout-scale-creep`. v8c1's `v8.text-gradient-wipe-reveal` (S04) is the same status-word reveal as `v8.text-status-type-on` (S07: the same word beside the agent name, revealed by a soft front at 3.5-3.7 f per letter; sheets/overview_00528-00573.jpg, sheets/overview_00912-00957.jpg) and is listed under that id. Curves were renamed to their final ids (curves/<technique>.csv for the main curve, .i2-.i6 for further distinct curves of the same technique; every sidecar keeps its chunk id as chunk_curve_id; the pre-merge set is in scratch/curves_premerge/); 110 curves remain after one duplicate was dropped (the f2160 callout collapse, same 20 f and shape rmse 0.020-0.026 as curves/v8.ui-callout-collapse-out.csv). The blur whip-reframes T04 and T05 keep the same UI on both sides, but the picture is replaced, so they stay boundaries (as the punch-in hard cuts T08 and T10 do).
Sources: tracker CSVs in scratch/v8c1_*.csv, scratch/v8c2_*.csv, scratch/v8c3_*.csv and scratch/v8c3r*_*.csv, scratch/v8c4_*.csv, scratch/v8c5r_*.csv, plus the coverage-gap review's re-measurements in scratch/gaps_*.csv and their follow-up in scratch/fix_*.csv (scratch/coverage_gaps_review.json); fitted curves in curves/; frame-numbered stills in type/ (marked still) and crops in crops/. Stills keep their chunk-local tags: type/v8c1-S01..S06 = S01-S06, type/v8c2-S01..S06 = S06-S11, type/v8c3-S02..S07 = S12-S17, type/v8c4-S1, S2, S3, S5, S6 = S17, S18, S19, S21, S22, type/v8c5-Sa, Sb, Sd, Se = S22, S24, S26, S27. Positions: x in % of picture width (1622 px), y in % of picture height (918 px); px values are picture pixels.
Uncertain (whole video): UNCERTAIN: f0-f3626 sound-sync offsets because the capture's audio delay is unknown and, after f3561, whether the captured audio looped with the picture · UNCERTAIN: f6-f42, f46-f56, f247-f340, f535-f576 reveal speeds because pictures arrive every 1-2 frames with +-1 frame jitter, so per-character rates carry about +-10%.

**3D:** none (flat 2D layers; the lockup group creeps larger about the frame centre over the static background).
**Graphic elements and UI:** mascot blobs with glyph eyes, rounded-square brand and app icons, thin x-shaped separator (1.7% W, luma 151), outlined tag pill (30.2% W x 8.7% H, fully rounded ends, ~2 px dark stroke). Mascots pop in staggered, pulse and swap eye glyphs while idle, drift inward from f60 and snap into the lockup centre.
**Speed changes:** lockup build f0-f60 in three overlapping eases (re-centre slide 21 f, label 2 letters 36 f, rise 20 f); hold with a linear creep f60-f112 (x1.016); mascots wind up slowly (f84-f115) and snap in (f115-f120); the zoom accelerates exponentially into the dissolve (scale x1.02 -> x3.14 in 17 f).
**Transition out -> S02:** T01, zoom-through with dissolve, 29 frames (f115-f143; S01 ends at floor((115+143)/2) = f129, so the last zoom pictures f130-f135 open S02); the lockup scales up about the frame centre (x 50.5%, y 50.0%) with growing motion blur, and from f136 the tunnel cross-dissolves in under the still-growing lockup fragments (tunnel darkest pixel luma 180 at f136, 112 at f138, 43 at f140, 0 at f143 in the centre region); shares: forward motion (the tunnel keeps flying toward its vanishing point near the frame centre), the six mascots (they reappear small at the tunnel's vanishing point), light luminance; curve: curves/v8.trans-zoom-through-dissolve.csv.
**Sound sync:** mascots start f2 = beat f14 -12; label 1 slide peak f14 = beat f14 +0 and strong onset f13 +1 (the strongest onset of f0-f733, 7.29); lockup rise f40 = beat f45 -5; tag f46 = beat f45 +1; convergence drift f84 = beat f79 +5; zoom f115 = beat f110 +5; dissolve f136 = beat f140 -4.
**Curves:** `curves/v8.icon-mascot-pop-in.csv` bezier(0.361, 2.110, 0.416, 0.738) D=20; spring {566.9, 21.35, mass 1}; rmse 0.032 / 0.051. `curves/v8.layout-lockup-recenter.csv` bezier(0.568, 0.224, 0.103, 1.006) D=26; spring {252.6, 26.98}; rmse 0.019 / 0.073. `curves/v8.layout-lockup-recenter.i2.csv` bezier(0.507, 0.009, 0.436, 0.825) D=23; spring {136.1, 18.41}; rmse 0.007 / 0.113. `curves/v8.text-tag-type-rise.csv` bezier(0.239, 0.969, 0.419, 1.091) D=30; spring {288.6, 30.24}; rmse 0.011 / 0.206 (from-value = first visible picture). `curves/v8.trans-zoom-through-dissolve.csv` (log2 scale) bezier(1.000, 0.399, 1.000, 0.410) D=15; spring {158.8, 58.66}; rmse 0.090 / 0.390 (ease-in only, to-value = last picture f132).
**Uncertain:** UNCERTAIN: f0-f26 opening wordmark spring and label settle easing because the recording starts mid-move (f0 already has vertical motion blur); values only · UNCERTAIN: f84-f124 mascot convergence easing because capture jitter (pictures f115/f116 one frame apart carry a 112 px step) and motion blur; bezier and spring fits had rmse > 0.07 and were dropped · UNCERTAIN: f115-f132 zoom-through curve shape metric because exponential ease-in: the fitted log2 curve matches every sample within 0.03 but the duration-normalised shape rmse is 0.090 because the built curve's 2% start comes 2 frames later than the samples'

### S02 · f130-f182 · MIXED · 0.88 s
**Layers (front to back):** the six mascots (flat, sharp) floating in a ring around the centre, flying out past the camera from f164; from f174 the footage card at the vanishing point (frame-aspect masked window carrying footage, 82 x 46 px = 5.1% W x 5.0% H at f174, centred x 49.9% / y 50.2%, soft zoom-blur halo; 68.6% W x 67.9% H by f182), the reason S02 is MIXED; 3D tunnel: a polygonal corridor of light panels with thin grey seam lines converging to a vanishing point near x 49%, y 51% (still f160), the walls carrying blurred greyscale photographic texture (dark door-like rectangles) that streams outward, paling to the white field around the card (f166-f179).

**Typography:** none.
**3D:** tunnel corridor in strong one-point perspective, camera dollying forward along its axis, no measurable roll (flow_rot noisy, UNCERTAIN); walls matte, evenly lit, with blurred photographic texture; mascots are flat sprites at different depths that grow as the camera passes them.
**Graphic elements and UI:** wall seam lines (thin grey), mascots with glyph eyes; from f174 the frame-aspect footage card (no visible border, edges and corners soft from the zoom's motion blur), the incoming half of T02.
**Speed changes:** mascots decelerate into a hover (f138-f150), drift (f150-f164), then accelerate past the camera (f164-f176); the footage card zooms with an expo-like ease-out from f174 (5.1% -> 68.6% W in 8 f).
**Transition out -> S03:** T02, tunnel-exit zoom-through, 37 frames (f164-f200; S02 ends at floor((164+200)/2) = f182, so the first footage-card pictures f174-f182 close S02 and make it MIXED), background swap at f174 (first picture with the footage card): the walls pale to white, the mascots fly out past the frame edges, a tiny footage card (82 x 46 px = 5.1% W x 5.0% H, centred x 49.9% / y 50.2%, with a soft zoom-blur halo about 3x its size) appears at the vanishing point and zooms toward full frame: 99.1% W by its bbox at f200 (T02's end and the zoom's 2% point, scratch/v8c1_card_scale_174-206.csv; white page margins of 8 / 4 / 5 / 7 px at the top / bottom / left / right), full width at f202, full frame at f206 (scratch/gaps_S03_card_edges_196-210.csv); shares: the frame centre (vanishing point = card centre), forward motion, the white field (luma 248) that becomes S03's background around the card; curve: curves/v8.footage-card-zoom-to-full.csv (incoming half; the outgoing fly-past is not fitted).
**Sound sync:** tunnel dissolve-in f136 = beat f140 -4; fly-past f164 = beat f169 -5; card appears f174 = beat f169 +5.

**3D:** none.
**Graphic elements and UI:** rounded message bubble, mascot avatar, check icon, wipe-revealed status label. The avatar's asterisk eyes glance left 14 px inside the static blob, hold and return (f552-f597); the blob itself does not move or rotate.
**Speed changes:** cut-in settle (fast-in / slow-out, 39 f), 15 f hold, then the slow ease-in pan that becomes the whip (36 f). After the cut the texts build on their own clocks: reply block fade (92% by f527) with its 3.3% H rise (23 f), name front f514-f530 with a slow darkening and sliding tail to f566, check f521-f532, status wipe f535-f576; the eye glance goes out in 14 f, holds 12 f and returns in 19 f.
**Transition out -> S05:** T04, blur whip-reframe, 65 frames (f570-f634) with the picture replaced between f606 and f608 (hidden hard cut f608; f607 repeats f606; S04 ends at floor((570+634)/2) = f602, so the last blurred close-up pictures f603-f607 open S05): the close-up accelerates right (ease-in 36 f) while blur grows (Laplacian sharpness 1896 at f594 -> 43 at f606, falling from f597; edge sigma 0.07% at f600 -> 0.34% W at f606), the cut zooms out to x0.66 (text-width ratios 0.643-0.672), and the wide panel arrives still moving right and decelerates (ease-out 26 f) while its blur clears (edge sigma 0.60% W at f608 -> baseline 0.10% at f625; sharp at f632); shares: the same chat content on the same white page, rightward motion across the cut; curves: curves/v8.trans-blur-whip-reframe.i2.csv (outgoing), curves/v8.trans-blur-whip-reframe.csv (incoming), curves/v8.trans-blur-whip-reframe.i3.csv (blur clear).
**Sound sync:** cut f512 = beat f511 +1; name and reply build f514 = beat f511 +3; check icon f521 = beat f511 +10; label wipe f535 = beat f541 -6; eye glance f552 = beat f541 +11 (strong onset f551 +1); whip starts f570 = beat f572 -2; hidden cut f608 = beat f604 +4.

**Transition out -> S06:** T05, blur whip-reframe, 59 frames (f668-f726; S05 ends at floor((668+726)/2) = f697) with the picture replaced between f696 and f700 (hidden hard cut f699; f697-f698 repeat f696). f699 is the first picture with incoming content, but only the incoming card layer: the x2.64 card (x 16.2%-83.0% W, y 7.8%-34.2% H) with the header label and search icon above it, at its close-up scale and 31-36 px higher than in f700, lies over the outgoing panel, which has moved another 16 px (1.7% H) down since f696 (f698 moved down 15.5-16 px matches f699 to 1.4 luma at the old bubble's right end and 0.6 below the card, 6.3 at the moving sidebar mascot, against 9.7 / 2.4 / 18.1 for f700); beside the card the old list card with its mascot (x 6-16% W) and the old bubble's right end (x 83-91% W) stay visible, below it the old agent row, reply and composer. So the incoming card leads the cut by one picture over the still-moving outgoing panel, and f700 is the first full close-up picture (scratch/v8c1_f699_layer_check.csv, scratch/v8c1_f699_layer_map.png). **3D:** none.
**Graphic elements and UI:** mascot avatars (dark 94 and mid-grey 149 fills), thin check icons, message bubble cut by the frame edge.
**Speed changes:** whip-in decelerates (12 -> 0.4 px/f in 20 f), slow drift that accelerates into the one-row step (peak 33.5 px/f), long settle (f962-f984), then an ease-in exit to 17 px/f.
**Transition out -> S08:** T07, vertical whip up, blur-through variant, 51 frames (f980-f1030) with the picture replaced between f1002 and f1004 (f1003 repeats f1002; hidden cut f1004 = c031); the same device as the f887-f934 whip (`v8.trans-whip-up`): the feed accelerates up (ease-in, 22 f, 17 px/f at f1002) under growing vertical motion blur (2 -> 24 px, plus a 3 px Gaussian at f1002), the whole frame blurs out (Laplacian sharpness 118 at f988 -> 5.0 at f1002), and the post card arrives 65 px (7.1% H) low (mean of both top corners), about 4% narrow, rising 8 px/f and defocused (Gaussian sigma 6.5 px = 0.40% W; sharpness 3.0 at f1004), sharpens in 6-8 f (sharpness 11 at f1007, 35 at f1008, 129 at f1010, 167 at f1014, about 200 from f1024) and settles with a strong ease-out, no overshoot, by f1030 (26 f by the 2% rule; the card bottom by f1024; the yaw that starts at f1024 lifts the left top corner but leaves the mean of both top corners at 79.0-80.5 px from f1030). The f914 whip has the same sharpness pattern (list 10 -> 1.3 at f912, chat 0.9 at f914 -> 62 at f920 -> 126 at f934; scratch/v8c2_T1_sharpness.csv). It differs only in the incoming half: an isotropic defocus (Sobel gy/gx 1.06 at f1004, card at rest 1.10) on a new composition instead of a vertical smear (gy/gx 0.27 at f916, chat at rest 0.66) on another view of the same app, and a 26 f settle instead of 20 f: a blur-through variant, possibly a second technique. Shares: upward motion, a blurred picture on both sides of the cut and the light page (248), while a dark card (fill 17) fills the middle. Segments tiled by the soft-transition rule: S07 ends at floor((980+1030)/2) = f1005 and S08 starts f1006, so the first card picture f1004 (repeated at f1005) closes S07, which stays GRAPHICS (the defocused card is the incoming half of the transition, as the first footage-card pictures f174-f182 close S02); detail/soft_980-1030 (the earlier window detail/soft_980-1034 covers it too); curves: curves/v8.cam-scroll-exit-up.csv (outgoing, S07 row 1 y), curves/v8.ui-card-rise-deblur-in.csv (incoming, mean of both top card corners); blur per picture in scratch/v8c2_T2_out_blur.csv (out), scratch/v8c2_S03_entry_blur.csv (in), scratch/v8c2_T2_sharpness.csv (both).
**Sound sync:** status word starts f920 = beat f911 +9; step starts f951 = beat f943 +8 (peak f955 +12); row 2 message f958 = +15; whip starts f980 = beat f973 +7; exit ramp f984 = beat f973 +11; hidden cut f1004 = beat f1005 -1; whip ends f1030 = beat f1033 -3; row 1 eye glance f932 = beat f943 -11.
**Curves:** `curves/v8.trans-whip-up.csv` bezier(0.169, 1.033, 0.910, 0.977) D=22; spring {305.3, 35.23, mass 1}; rmse 0.015 / 0.080. `curves/v8.ui-list-step-scroll.csv` bezier(0.272, 0.451, 0.068, 0.901) D=23; spring {364.7, 45.47}; rmse 0.017 / 0.065. `curves/v8.cam-scroll-exit-up.csv` bezier(0.348, 0.104, 0.814, 0.084) D=23; rmse 0.011 (ease-in only, to-value = last picture).
**Uncertain:** UNCERTAIN: f1004-f1007 status pill drop-in start because the pill is absent at f1004 and f1005-f1006 repeat f1004; the first picture f1007 is taken as the start of the move, any motion before it is not visible (the path-distance fit from there has rmse 0.005)

**3D:** post card yaws to 26.5 deg with its left edge toward the camera (derived from the measured left/right edge heights; pinhole focal length fitted per frame 2315-2800 px, mean 2618 px = about 34 deg horizontal field of view), then recedes to depth scale 0.80 while sliding left. The deck is a row of about 22 flat matte cards stacked in depth, seen from the right so each card behind shows a sliver to the left of the next; the stack recedes strongly (its far end at the left frame edge is 0.48-0.57 x the front card's height, f1164-f1172); no depth-of-field blur, only motion blur on arrivals and fast camera moves; no specular light. The stack collapses into the front card as the view returns to front-on (f1223-f1230). The status pill lies in the card plane (foreshortens with it) and keeps a residual -5 deg tilt on re-entry. Environment: from f1074 to f1200 a dot grid on the page behind the cards; it is denser at the centre (nearest-neighbour spacing 41 px) than toward the edges (45-46 px at the top and bottom, 49-52 px at the right, 57-58 px at the left; horizontal neighbours 63-74 px apart at the side edges against 49-57 px vertically), its rows bow outward at their ends, and it drifts left at the same rate per local spacing everywhere: consistent with a flat grid seen through a wide-angle (pincushion-like) projection or a grid on a curved surface around the camera (geometry UNCERTAIN). Its spacing and drift ignore the deck's zoom and push, so it is a separate screen-space background layer, not part of the cards' 3D space.
**Graphic elements and UI:** post cards (avatar circle, name, handle, message lines, photo panel, stats row of 4 icons + counters), status pill + mascot, mascot eye states, a background dot grid (f1074-f1200: fades in over 20 f, drifts left at a constant rate, switches off within one picture at f1202), a shimmer band that sweeps the status pill each time it comes to rest (f1042-f1057, f1240-f1254): a page-white (248) diagonal band 2.5 x the pill height wide (354 / 370 px on pills 142 / 150 px tall) that grows out of the pill's left end, crosses it with an ease-in-out and leaves through the right end, clipped to the pill fill.
**Speed changes:** slow turn start (f1024-f1050 under 9 deg), faster middle (f1054-f1066), card recede/slide accelerating 3 -> 11 px/f into the deck; the deck runs at a constant 15 cards per second while the camera zooms in, out (to f1140) and then pushes in with a strong ease-in into the oversized landing (f1174), followed by an ease-out pull-back (f1176-f1220), a 7-frame collapse, a hold and a slow ease-in drift up into the cut. Under the deck the dot grid keeps one constant drift (2.7-4.6 px/f by region) from f1074 to its switch-off at f1202, whatever the camera does.
**Transition out -> S09:** T08, hard cut f1266 (c047), a punch-in on the same card: the close-up is x2.5 larger (template scale 0.40 +-0.01) on the card's lower left (photo panel bottom + stats row); shares: the card itself, the status pill + mascot (new label, a lighter mascot, fill 149), and upward motion (the card drifts up 4.5 px/f into the cut, the close-up rises 23.5 px/f after it).
**Sound sync:** cut f1004 = beat f1005 -1; pill appears f1007 = +2; dot grid fade-in f1074 = beat f1061 +13 (full f1094 = beat f1088 +6); dot grid off f1202 = beat f1210 -8 (strong onset f1209 -7); turn starts f1024-f1026 = beat f1033 -7; shimmer 1 starts f1042 = beat f1033 +9; deck starts f1094 = beat f1088 +6; push-in f1140 = beat f1148 -8; landing f1174 = beat f1178 -4; collapse f1222 = beat f1210 +12 (strong onset f1209 +13); rest f1230 = beat f1237 -7; shimmer 2 starts f1240 = beat f1237 +3; cut f1266 = beat f1267 -1.

**3D:** none in the segment (the turn into 3D starts in the transition out).
**Graphic elements and UI:** product page UI as above; the counter digits do not change over f1997-f2010; the arrow cursor (stills type/v8c3-S06-cursor_f01998.png, type/v8c3-S06-cursor_f02010.png).
**Speed changes:** fast push landing (peak about 108 px/f) with a strong ease-out that never comes to rest: the content still rises 11, 8, 5 px per picture at f2006-f2010 while the page's mask already insets (5 -> 16 -> 34 px a side), and the radial-blur shrink takes over at f2012. No hold: the push and the zoom-out overlap by about 4 f.
**Transition out -> S17:** T16, soft zoom-out morph, 27 f (f2009-f2035), detail/soft_2009-2035. It starts under the push's landing: the page's rounded-rect mask insets from f2006 (34-35 px a side by f2010) while the content is unscaled and still rising; from f2012 the page itself shrinks x0.53 into the front card of a 3D screen stack with strong radial zoom blur (30% of the scale change between f2012 and f2014), keeps its centre y at 460-502 px (50-55% H), starts to yaw (right side receding) from about f2022, and screen layers appear behind it from f2026-f2028 and fan out (S17). Shares: the same element (the page becomes the stack's front layer) on the same light page. Curve: curves/v8.trans-zoom-out-morph.csv.
**Sound sync:** page enters f1985 = beat f1985 +0; cursor f1997 = +12; mask inset starts f2006 = beat f2019 -13; content shrink with radial blur f2012 = -7.
**Curves:** `curves/v8.trans-push-up.csv` bezier(0.2077, 0.8144, 0.4782, 0.8899) D=20; spring {366.915, 51.658, mass 1}; rmse 0.0073 / 0.089. `curves/v8.trans-zoom-out-morph.csv` bezier(0.6111, 0.0115, 0.0029, 0.4987) D=24; spring {227.837, 28.952, mass 1}; rmse 0.0214 / 0.0716.
**Uncertain:** UNCERTAIN: f2004, f2012-f2020 cursor position on blurred pictures and its scale during the zoom-out because the template is lost on the blurred pictures

**3D:** (f2023-f2192) a stack of 10-12 flat screen layers (count from still type/v8c3-S07-stack_f02100.png; edges overlap) built from the S16 page: the page shrinks and yaws (right side receding) and the other layers slide out behind it toward the upper right, each offset up and to the right; perspective is weak: the silhouette heights at the near and far ends are about equal (235-240 px = 26% H at f2101), the far end sits 58 px higher; yaw angle UNCERTAIN; no measurable depth of field (Laplacian variance 3184 front, 2080 middle, 3633 far, still f2100); flat matte screens; no lighting change on the layers; the soft dark halo around the stack builds with the fan-out (see Movements); camera: the slow pull-back above. Entry: the zoom-out morph; exit: the push-in and flatten T17. · (f2193-f2235) screen-layer stack: about 11 flat cards spaced along a diagonal that recedes to the upper right, each card yawed so its front side turns toward the left; strong perspective (front card's left edge 213 px = 23.2% H tall at f2208); depth of field softens the far layers; matte cards with a large soft grey shadow on the page. Yaw angle UNCERTAIN (the cards' true aspect and their right edges are hidden). The stack recedes slowly (-1% in 16 f) until T17.
**Graphic elements and UI:** (f2023-f2192) callout pills with mascot icons of different shapes (5 seen on still f2100, each with glyph eyes); leader lines (2-3 px, light) with white anchor dots (12.2 px): in each callout the dot pops first, its line then draws out toward the pill's slot and the pill pops while the line draws; on the way out the pill collapses first, its line retracts into the dot, and the dot shrinks and fades last; the halo, which builds with the fan-out (f2028-f2050, then two steps to its final level by about f2080). · (f2193-f2235) callouts (mascot icon, 3-word label, near-white pill, 2 px light leader line, white anchor dot), header title, screen layers, grey shadow ellipse.
**Speed changes:** (f2023-f2192) fast fan-out with an ease-out (overshoot-like y2 1.24 in the fit) while the halo builds near-linearly under it (about 0.045 of its first level per frame), then a rolling, irregular callout rhythm over a slow linear pull-back: each callout is a 3-step chain (dot 5-12 f; line 8-14 f, starting 5-10 f after the dot; pill 18 f, starting 0-4 f after the line), pill specks 4, 6, 8, 14, 26, 12 f apart and dots 0-25 f apart; collapses 18-21 f apart run the chain backwards (pill 20 f, line about 14 f from 4-6 f later, dot last). · (f2193-f2235) each callout swells 5-7.5% over 7-8 f, holds, then collapses with an accelerating ease-in; the collapses come faster one after another (starts f2190, f2204, f2214: gaps 14, 10 f); the stack's slow recede turns into the accelerating T17 push at f2212; the header title's fade starts slowly (about 0.011 opacity per frame f2225-f2234) and speeds up to 0.03-0.05 per frame until the page covers it.
**Transition out -> S18:** T17 morph (camera push-in + yaw to flat), 47 frames (f2212-f2258), anchor c071 (f2252). The far end of the stack swings out first (right extent 1272 -> 1325 px = 81.7% W and top edge 262 -> 246 px over f2210-f2220; the layers then separate), while the camera pushes in and the layers turn toward the camera; the front card's left edge holds at 379 px (23.4% W) until f2218, then accelerates left: 373 (f2227) -> 336 (f2236) -> 261 (f2242) -> 180 (f2246) -> 114 (f2250) -> 79 (f2255) -> 64 (f2258) -> 60 px = 3.7% W (f2262) (scratch/v8c4_T1_frontcard_edge.csv); its left-edge height grows 213 px (f2208) -> 313 (f2236) -> 544 (f2246) -> 705 px = 76.8% H (f2250) (scratch/v8c4_T1_frontcard_leftedge.csv) and the flat card measures 1492 x 840 px (92.0% W x 91.5% H) at f2258 (corner radius about 30 px, measured at f2300); the layers behind slide in behind it (stack width 896 -> 1288 px f2210-f2242, front card alone from about f2250). The stack is visibly soft f2236-f2250 and sharp again by f2255 (detail/soft_2212-2258; Laplacian variance of the stack region 429 -> 85 -> 232 at f2230 / f2246 / f2255, scratch/v8c4_T1_sharpness.csv, partly a content change); the grey shadow fades with the push (left-edge 10-90% width 118 px at f2212 -> 30 px f2242 -> 6 px f2256 -> 2 px f2260, scratch/v8c4_T1_stack_bbox.csv). Shares: the front screen card itself becomes the flat page at the top of S18 (same content, same light page 248); the scroll of S18 starts at f2256 before the flatten has fully settled. While the page lands, a toast fades in on it (f2246-f2258) and an arrow cursor resolves on its countdown card (f2250); both ride the page into S18 (see S18). Curve: curves/v8.trans-stack-push-flatten.csv.
**Sound sync:** (f2023-f2192) label f2017 = beat f2019 -2; halo build from f2028 = +9, its first level f2050 = beat f2054 -4, its steps f2059 = +5 and f2073 = beat f2084 -11; first dots f2031 = beat f2019 +12, first pill speck f2036 = +17; bottom-centre pill speck f2054 = beat f2054 +0; callout 6 dot f2084 = beat f2084 +0, its line f2092 = +8, its pill f2094 = +10; callout 7 dot f2096 = weak onset f2096 +0 (beat f2084 +12), its pill f2106 = beat f2115 -9; collapses about f2103 = -12, about f2124 = +9, about f2142 = beat f2142 +0, f2160 = beat f2167 -7 (its line from about f2165 = -2). · (f2193-f2235) B collapse starts f2190 = beat f2198 -8; C f2204 = +6; A f2214 = +16 (half a beat); T17 starts f2212 = +14; header fade starts f2225-f2227 = beat f2231 -6 / -4 (first changed picture f2227); T17 ends f2258 = beat f2262 -4.

**3D:** none (flat UI).
**Graphic elements and UI:** flattened page band with its thumbnail row (selected thumbnail = light rounded chip), toast on the band (rounded rectangle, check icon in a white circle, 1-line label), arrow cursor on the band's countdown card (stills type/v8c4-S2-cursor_f02256.png, type/v8c4-S2-cursor_f02264.png, type/v8c4-S2-cursor_f02276.png), 4-lobed mascot avatar with arc eyes, check icon, attachment card (document icon, download icon), shimmer band.
**Speed changes:** scroll fast-in / long slow-out (ease-out tail into a 0.08% H/f linear drift); the headline streams at a steady 2.1 f per letter with one 3 f pause; the avatar pop overshoots 19.5% and undershoots 3.5%; the drift ramps into the accelerating exit (T18) from f2367. On the band: the toast fades in with a linear ramp (about 0.085 per frame) as T17 lands, rides the scroll and the drift, then fades out with a slow start while it slides 10 px down against the page (16 f, gone 5-7 f into T18); the cursor glides down-right on the page at a near-constant 2.2-3.2 px/f while the scroll (up to 36 px/f) carries it out of the frame top (f2280-f2284). Against the toast, the cursor seems to start fast (about 10 px/f f2255-f2258) and slow down: that is the page's own flatten (its right part still widens 1343 -> 1371 px over f2255-f2262 while the toast side holds), not the cursor.
**Transition out -> S19:** T18 push-up, 74 frames (f2367-f2440), picture replaced between f2402 and f2404 (f2403 repeats f2402; hidden cut f2404, missed by all three detectors; f2404 shows only a faint sliver at the bottom edge, dark rows y 890-900). Outgoing half: out of the 0.73 px/f drift the whole block (avatar, status row, headline, sub line, card) and the band above it accelerate up with growing vertical motion blur: 1.5 px/f (f2372) -> 3 (f2378) -> 9 (f2394) -> 12.5 (f2398) -> about 43 px/f at the last picture f2402 (headline centre 40.96% -> 15.58% H; scratch/v8c4_S2_headline_word1.csv); no settle. Incoming half: see S19. Shares: upward motion with vertical blur, the same light page and the same chat-block grid (avatar column 10.3% W, text column 15.9% W); the incoming headline settles at 42.7% H, where the outgoing headline sat (41.0% H) when its exit began. Curves: curves/v8.trans-push-up.i4.csv (outgoing), curves/v8.trans-push-up.i3.csv (incoming). Detail: detail/soft_2367-2440.
**Sound sync:** scroll starts f2256 = beat f2262 -6; card appears f2274 = +12; avatar first picture f2294 = beat f2291 +3 (peak f2300 +9); status row f2304 = +13; shimmer f2311 = beat f2318 -7; headline done f2312 = -6; sub line done f2328 = +10; exit ramp f2367 = beat f2373 -6; hidden cut f2404 = beat f2404 +0 (an onset also sits at f2404); toast fade-in starts f2246-f2247 = beat f2231 +15 (half a beat), full f2258 = beat f2262 -4; cursor first seen f2250 = -12, out of the frame top f2280-f2284 = beat f2291 -11 / -7; toast fade-out starts f2358 = beat f2346 +12, gone f2372-f2374 = beat f2373 -1 / +1.
**Curves:** `curves/v8.cam-scroll-up-settle.csv` bezier(0.351, 0.153, 0.099, 1.000) D=51 (built 39 f); spring {77.2, 17.11, mass 1}; rmse 0.015 / 0.057. `curves/v8.ui-card-stretch-in-blur.csv` bezier(0.132, 1.057, 1.000, 0.968) D=23; spring {255.9, 32.34}; rmse 0.049 / 0.078 (visible part only). Avatar pop-in: no own curve (both fits fail the shape rule, see the avatar row); the technique's curve is the S01 one, `curves/v8.icon-mascot-pop-in.csv` bezier(0.361, 2.110, 0.416, 0.738) D=20; spring {566.9, 21.35}; rmse 0.032 / 0.051. `curves/v8.fx-shimmer-sweep.i2.csv` bezier(0.171, 0.030, 0.741, 0.838) D=33; rmse 0.022. `curves/v8.trans-push-up.i4.csv` bezier(0.687, 0.005, 1.000, 0.764) D=40 (built 33 f); rmse 0.022 (ease-in only, to-value = last picture f2402). `curves/v8.ui-toast-fade-slide-out.csv` bezier(0.689, 0.623, 0.501, 1.038) D=17 (built 14 f); spring {157.6, 17.58, mass 1}; rmse 0.037 / 0.312 (toast opacity 1 -> 0, active f2358-f2374). Toast fade-in: no curve (both fits fail the shape rule, see its row; a linear ramp of about 0.085 per frame, f2246-f2258). Cursor glide: no curve (near-constant speed, no rest in view).

**Typography:** none.
**3D:** none (flat sprites; the outward bursts plus the shrinking sizes read as a zoom-out, but the positions spread radially rather than scale about the centre).
**Graphic elements and UI:** mascot icons only (about 9 shapes, dot or chevron eyes, motion-smeared eyes during the fast phases f2540-f2547 and f2578-f2586).
**Speed changes:** the split pair comes out oversized (x1.50) and shrinks back with an ease-out from full speed in 15 f while it separates, both ending at f2522; each burst = ease-in outward flight (16 and 14 f), a one-picture appearance of new mascots, a small overshoot (+11%, then +3.5%) and a 16-12 f settle; holds of 6, 4 and 6 f; bursts start 40 f apart and the implosion 36 f after burst 2, so the rhythm tightens slightly.
**Transition out -> S21:** T20 morph (converge + fly to slot), 67 frames (f2604-f2670). The field implodes into the centre (above, radial motion blur f2612-f2620), the cluster shrinks to one tiny light mascot (f2632), holds and drifts slightly (f2632-f2637), then flies to the avatar slot of the next chat block on a dipping arc: x 49.69% (f2632) -> 47.32 (f2642) -> 43.48 (f2648) -> 32.89 (f2654) -> 19.67 (f2658) -> 14.01 (f2662) -> 10.99 (f2670) -> 10.25% W (f2682); y 50.6 -> lowest 56.6% H (f2654) -> 38.1 (f2670) -> 33.7% H (f2690, riding the block's rise); peak about 55 px/f (f2651-f2656; 50 px/f mean over f2651-f2658) with its eyes smeared horizontally (f2648-f2658); size 40 -> 80 px (x2.0) by f2682 (scratch/v8c4_T5_mascot_path.csv). Shares: the mascot itself (light rounded triangle with chevron eyes) becomes S21's avatar; the light page stays; S21's status row and headline stream in during the flight. Curves: curves/v8.particle-mascot-converge.csv, curves/v8.trans-mascot-converge-fly.csv. Detail: detail/soft_2604-2670.
**Sound sync:** pair largest f2507 = beat f2497 +10; split and scale-back settle f2522 = beat f2527 -5; burst 1 starts f2528 = +1; ring appears f2547 = beat f2555 -8 (onset f2548 -1); burst 2 starts f2568 = strong onset f2569 -1; new mascots f2584 = beat f2584 +0; burst 2 settles f2598 = beat f2610 -12; implosion starts f2604 = beat and strong onset f2610 -6; merge f2623 = +13.
**Curves:** `curves/v8.particle-mascot-split.csv` bezier(0.121, 1.174, 0.982, 0.965) D=23 (built 17 f); spring {430.8, 65.79}; rmse 0.010 / 0.051. `curves/v8.particle-mascot-split.i2.csv` bezier(0.000, 0.618, 0.896, 1.008) D=16 (built 15 f); spring {321.0, 79.81}; rmse 0.016 / 0.097 (pair scale x1.50 -> x1.0, active f2507-f2522; it starts at full speed, so only the bezier fits). `curves/v8.particle-mascot-burst-ring.csv` bezier(0.833, 0.531, 0.214, 1.592) D=25; spring {228.6, 15.48}; rmse 0.028 / 0.375 (burst 2, outermost centre distance). `curves/v8.particle-mascot-converge.csv` bezier(0.876, 0.083, 0.513, 0.445) D=20 (built 17 f); spring {247.8, 22.96}; rmse 0.004 / 0.455 (ends at full speed, no settle).

**Typography:** none designed (text appears only inside the footage).
**3D:** none.
**Graphic elements and UI:** portrait footage cards in a marquee row.
**Speed changes:** hard-braking entrance (291 -> 7.37 px/f in 64 f: within 2% of the braking curve at f3286, then 11 -> 7.5 px/f until the steady marquee speed at f3306), a constant marquee (7.37 px/f), then an ease-in drop downward during which the horizontal scroll stops without easing (full speed to f3374, still at f3376).
**Transition out -> S26:** T25, whip down with a hidden two-step cut, 47 frames (f3348-f3394): the row sinks while still scrolling left with a strong ease-in (top edge 6.3% H at f3342 -> 7.3% f3351 -> 11.1% f3360 -> 21.2% f3370 -> 30.2% f3374 -> 37.8% f3376 -> 54.1% H f3379; 50 px/f at the end). Step 1, background: between the pictures f3376 and f3379 (f3377-f3378 repeat f3376, so the grid frame of the swap within f3377-f3379 is not resolved) the flat white page under the row is replaced by S26's end-card background (darker top-right and bottom-left gradient, faint rounded outline arcs, dot grid), a full swap, not a blend: the mean luma of x 1300-1600, y 0-40 is 248.0 in every picture f3300-f3376 and 227.8 from f3379 on (the end card's value), and above the row f3379 equals f3380 to 0.010 luma mean abs diff (99th percentile 0, max 5); so for one picture, f3379, the row sits on the new background (crops/crop_0_0_1622x520_03374-03382.jpg, detail/soft_3348-3394 f3376 vs f3379). Step 2, foreground: at f3380 the row is removed (c096) in the same picture as the end card's brand mark icon arrives from above the top edge moving down (f3380 only its lower 107 px at the top edge; centre 31.0% H f3382, 44.6% f3384), overshoots 42 px (4.6% H) below its rest (49.0% H f3386-f3388) and springs back to 44.4% H by f3398. Shares: downward motion; the last outgoing picture f3379 already carries the end card's background, identical to the first incoming picture f3380 everywhere above the row. Segments split at floor((3348+3394)/2) = f3371 (S25 ends f3371, S26 starts f3372): the window f3348-f3394 is the measured active range of both halves (outgoing row f3348-f3379; incoming end card: background from f3379, icon drop f3380-f3394), so the two-step cut f3379/f3380 falls after the midpoint and the row's last sinking pictures f3372-f3379 open S26, as T04 and T05 are tiled. Curves: curves/v8.trans-whip-down.i4.csv (outgoing), curves/v8.trans-whip-down.i3.csv (incoming); detail/soft_3348-3394.
**Sound sync:** cut f3242 = beat f3242 +0; steady marquee from f3306 = beat f3295 +11; sinking starts f3344 = beat f3352 -8; horizontal stop f3375 = beat f3381 -6; background swap f3379 = beat f3381 -2; row removed f3380 = beat f3381 -1.
**Curves:** `curves/v8.layout-marquee-whip-in.csv` bezier(0.000, 0.527, 0.014, 1.001) D=61 (built 42 f); spring {92.2, 22.53, mass 1}; rmse 0.027 / 0.176 (sub-pixel row position minus the measured 7.37 px/f drift, new pictures only; the fitted bezier is within 0.004 of every sample except the pictures stamped one frame late: f3253 0.021, f3281 0.007, f3295 0.005, f3323 0.0045). `curves/v8.trans-whip-down.i4.csv` bezier(0.606, 0.039, 0.909, 0.258) D=35; rmse 0.013 (ease-in only, to-value = last picture f3379; spring not usable, 0.480).

## Motion personality
A product film that never stops moving: almost every layout arrives already in motion and leaves by accelerating out, so each exit is a long ease-in (20-84 f) that passes its speed and direction to the next layout, which lands with a strong ease-out (19-29 f) or a springy overshoot (mascot pops to x1.17-1.22, dropped icons and words 4.3-5.2% H past their rest), and the changes rotate through whips with hidden cuts (up, down, left, re-framing zoom), pushes, punch-in cuts on motion and shared-element morphs in which one element (a card, an avatar, a mascot swarm, a 3D screen stack) carries the eye into the next scene. Inside a held view the build is stepped and staggered: labels and messages stream in left to right behind soft trailing fronts, lists and threads step up one row on ease-in-out scrolls (the S06 additions accelerate from 28 to 8 f apart), status pills drop or grow in and get a page-white shimmer when they come to rest, and the picture keeps creeping (linear scale creeps, slow pull-backs, camera push-ins with long settles). Small life runs underneath everything: blob mascots pop with a springy overshoot, glance their glyph eyes sideways inside still bodies, swap eye glyphs, converge, split and burst, and motion blur appears only on what moves fast and clears within a few frames of each landing.
